### Raiders of the Pindus

| name | ths\_raiders\_of\_the\_pindus\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Raiders of the Pindus |
| description | Survival in Northern Greece requires a departure from traditional Byzantine field tactics in favor of a sophisticated system of partisan warfare. The Thessalian military doctrine will evolve to focus on *Klephthic* tactics, utilizing the deep ravines and dense forests of the Pindus mountains to bleed enemy supply lines dry through constant harassment. This cultural shift toward guerilla resistance meant that any occupying force will face a landscape where every shepherd is a scout and every mountain path a potential ambush site, making the conquest of Thessaly a logistical nightmare for foreign empires. |
| icon | Advance defensive mentality.png |

| name | gre\_rebuilding\_the\_theme\_system\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Rebuilding the Theme System |
| description | We have looked to the golden age of the Middle Byzantine period to solve our manpower crisis. By granting hereditary land plots to *Akritai* (border guards) in exchange for perpetual military service, we create a self-sustaining defense force deeply rooted in the soil they protect. ### Reclaiming the Peloponnese

| name | moe\_reclaiming\_the\_peloponnese\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Reclaiming the Peloponnese |
| description | Following the Byzantine recovery of the forts of Monemvasia, Maina, and Mystras, the Despotate of the Morea has dedicated itself to a long 'reconquista' of the peninsula from the Frankish Principalities. This campaign is not merely one of siegecraft, but of cultural subversion, as the Greek administration encourages the local population to rise against their Latin lords in favor of the 'rightful ruler'. Through a combination of strategic marriages, persistent skirmishing, and the gradual absorption of feudal fiefs, the Morea slowly knits together a fractured land into a cohesive province. |

### Reform of the Communal Councils

| name | ligurian\_communal\_councils |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Reform of the Communal Councils |
| description | The self-governing communes that had managed Ligurian harbor life for centuries were gradually formalized and rationalized, replacing informal custom with written charters, elected committees, and standardized administrative procedures that reduced the friction of local governance. These reformed councils gave the merchant class a structured forum for managing the affairs of their communities without dependence on noble patronage or distant judicial authority, embedding a civic competence in the institutions of every Ligurian town. This administrative tradition gives our government an unusual depth of trained civic talent and makes our institutions more responsive to the needs of the burghers who sustain our commerce. |
| icon | Advance administrative leadership.png |

### Reform the Dīwān

| name | reform\_the\_diwan |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Reform the Dīwān |
| description | We are fortunate to be served by loyal and well-educated Farsi administrators, who have perfected the art of governance for generations. Sadly, however, a hereditary office is one that over time might stagnate and lack innovation. By reducing the number of inherited offices with each generation, we can make sure that we reward not only loyalty, but also ability. Able administrators must rise to high positions regardless of which family they come from. |

### Reformation of the Patriarchate

| name | reformation\_of\_the\_bulgarian\_patriarchate |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Reformation of the Patriarchate |
| description | The spiritual heart of the Bulgarian world will undergo a rigorous and profound transformation under the iron-willed guidance of our Patriarch. Recognizing that the survival of the nation depends upon the purity of its faith and the precision of its holy texts, the Patriarchate will initiate a sweeping linguistic and liturgical reform centered at the Holy Trinity Monastery of Tarnovo. By purging the Church Slavonic manuscripts of centuries of scribal errors and corrupt localisms, we will establish a new, standardized orthography that will become the gold standard for the entire Orthodox Slavic world, from the Danube to the Volga. |

| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Religious Pluralism |

### Religious Pragmatism

| name | glh\_religious\_pragmatism |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Religious Pragmatism |
| description | Constraining, inflexible doctrine has never won many admirers among the Tatar hordes. When the Great Öz Beg Khān adopted Islam, it was the faith of a Sufi Shaykh or a zealous Qāḍī that he invited into his lands. Centuries of tradition and loyalty are what we would rather build our state upon. Religion has a part to play, but we would prefer to see our country thrive than to fall apart in the chaos of forced religious homogeneity. |

| --- | --- |

Philosophers from around the world have collaborated to create a document entitled the 'Declaration of the Rights of Man and of the Citizen', proclaiming that these rights are universal and should be enforced by law. ### Rétablissement

| name | retablissement |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Rétablissement |
| description | After tumultuous wars, a new era dawns upon Saxony — the remarkable Saxon *Rétablissement*. Amidst the ruins of our past, our nation will rise like a phoenix, rebuilding its shattered economy and charting a path toward prosperity. Driven by a blend of visionary state reforms and disciplined spending, our nation will emerge as a commercial hub. With unparalleled administrative prowess and a strategic focus on economic growth, this resolute transformation will mark a pivotal moment in history, shaping the destiny of Saxony well into the future. |
